# Code quality audit

Read-only audit of `monopoly-bank` at commit `05d52ec` (branch `feat/bank-to-player-limit`, clean tree), 2026-10-17. No source or test file was changed. Every number below was measured with the tool named next to it; anything that could not be measured is listed under [Could not verify](#could-not-verify).

**How it was measured.** All tools ran in a throwaway clone of the repository outside the working tree, so no config, dependency or report was added to the repo. Tools: ESLint 10.9 with the repo's `eslint.config.js` plus a temporary rule block and `eslint-plugin-sonarjs` 4.2.2; `jscpd` 5.4.1; Vitest with `@vitest/coverage-v8`; Stryker 10.0.0 with `@stryker-mutator/vitest-runner` 10.0.0; and two small TypeScript-AST scanners for if/else chains, guard ladders, nested loops, nested ternaries and assertion patterns in tests. Node 24.19.0 was the only runtime available (the repo pins Node 22).

**Size of the code base.** 203 production `.ts`/`.tsx` files and 157 test files. The suite has 158 test files and 1,596 tests, all passing (Vitest 5.0.0, 33 s).

No if/else chain reaches 4 branches; branching is written as guard ladders instead (10 ladders of 5 to 20 guards).                                                                                                                     | Medium   |

## 1. Structure and size

### 1.1 Files over 250 lines

70 tracked files are over 250 lines: 35 production files and 35 test or e2e files (`wc -l`). The complete list, each with a one-line reason, is in [Appendix A1 and A2](#a1-production-files-over-250-lines-35). The ten largest production files:

| File                                  | Lines | Why it is large                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------- | ----: | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/i18n/translations.ts`            | 1,771 | Both full catalogs (English and Ukrainian) in one module.                                                |
| `shared/domain/banking.ts`            | 1,600 | 24 exported operations, 24 error classes (`:152-1343`) and 29 interface/type declarations in one module. |
| `worker/services/banking-service.ts`  | 1,349 | One class orchestrates every banking command, payment and trade request, loans, reversal and bankruptcy. |
| `src/pages/game/BankingDialog.tsx`    | 1,314 | One component for 14 action types: 25 `useState`, 62 `action === '…'` checks.                            |
| `src/pages/GamePage.tsx`              |   991 | Page layout plus WebSocket lifecycle, 13 API client calls and response merging.                          |
| `worker/routes/api-router.ts`         |   963 | 44 route conditions in one if-ladder, plus rate limiting and join/finish orchestration.                  |
| `worker/game-session.ts`              |   836 | Socket accept, command dedupe, authorization, auction alarms and broadcast in one class.                 |
| `src/pages/CreateGamePage.tsx`        |   787 | Settings, house rules, players, colour picker and draft persistence; 17 `useState`.                      |
| `shared/contracts/api.ts`             |   692 | 84 type declarations (types only, no logic).                                                             |
| `worker/validation/api-validation.ts` |   643 | 52 parser functions for every request body.                                                              |

## 2. Complexity and nesting

### 2.1 ESLint run with a temporary configuration

The temporary flat config extended the repo's own `eslint.config.js` and added, for `src/**`, `worker/**` and `shared/**`: `complexity` 10, `max-depth` 3, `max-nested-callbacks` 3, `max-lines-per-function` 40, `max-lines` 250, `sonarjs/cognitive-complexity` 15. It lived only in the scratch clone. No other rule fired.

| Rule                                    | Production code | Test files |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------: | ---------: |
| `complexity` (max 10)                   |              78 |          0 |
| `sonarjs/cognitive-complexity` (max 15) |              31 |          0 |
| `max-depth` (max 3)                     |               6 |          0 |
| `max-nested-callbacks` (max 3)          |               0 |         22 |
| `max-lines-per-function` (max 40)       |             117 |        198 |
| `max-lines` (max 250)                   |              34 |         31 |
| **Total**                               |         **266** |    **251** |

### 2.2 if/else chains, guard ladders and switches that should be lookups

The AST scanner found **no** `if / else if` chain of 4 or more branches anywhere in production code. Branching is written as consecutive `if (…) return`/`throw` guards and as `switch` statements over the transaction-type union. The scanner found 10 ladders of 5 or more consecutive guards and 12 switches:

| Location                                                                                                                        | Shape                                                                                                                                              | Proposed replacement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `worker/routes/api-router.ts:155-179`, `:234-252`, `:330-560` (20 guards), `:610-710`                                           | `if (pathname === … && method === …)` ladders (44 route conditions in total) after 23 regexes are executed up front for every request (`:127-152`) | A route table `const routes: Route[] = [{ method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/games\/([^/]+)\/start$/, handler: startGame }]` matched by `matchRoute(routes, request.method, pathname)`, with handlers grouped in `worker/routes/{auth,games,banking,auctions,requests}-routes.ts`. This also removes the depth-4 block at `:585`. |
| `worker/game-session.ts:372-394` (5 guards) and `:401-435` (9 guards), both in `execute`                                        | `if (command.type === 'X') return { status, data: await service.x(…) }`                                                                            | `const commandHandlers: { [K in LiveMutationCommand['type']]: (command: Extract<LiveMutationCommand, { type: K }>) => Promise<ResponsePayload> }`, so `tsc` keeps it exhaustive.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `worker/game-session.ts:454-482` (5 guards, `authorizeMutation`)                                                                | Which role or wallet must authorise each command type                                                                                              | `const authorizeFor: Record<LiveMutationCommand['type'], (command) => Promise<void>>`, sharing one `requireRequestController(kind, id, side)` for the four payment/trade request cases.                                                                                                                                          |
| `shared/contracts/live.ts:90-130`                                                                                               | 15 `command.type === …` disjuncts followed by a 4-level ternary on the payload (`:120`)                                                            | `const commandPayloadGuards: Record<LiveMutationCommand['type'], (value: object) => boolean>`; the function becomes `isUuid(commandId) && type in commandPayloadGuards && commandPayloadGuards[type](value)`.                                                                                                                    |
| `shared/contracts/live.ts:146-165` (8 guards)                                                                                   | Event-type guards                                                                                                                                  | The same `Record<LiveServerEvent['type'], guard>` table.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `src/components/TableCalculator.tsx:44-54` (10 guards)                                                                          | Key → action                                                                                                                                       | `const KEY_ACTIONS: Record<string, CalculatorAction> = { '.': { type: 'decimal' }, ',': { type: 'decimal' }, '+': …, Backspace: …, Escape: … }`, keeping only the digit regex and the `Enter` special case as guards.                                                                                                            |
| `shared/domain/auction.ts:123-127`                                                                                              | 5 ordered bid checks                                                                                                                               | Keep as guards (the order is the contract tested in `auction.test.ts › bids applies the checks in order`); no change.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `shared/domain/player-control.ts:12` (17 cases), `shared/domain/reversal.ts:47` (20), `shared/domain/round-tracking.ts:26` (10) | Switches mapping a transaction type to a debited, credited or initiator side                                                                       | `const AUTHORITY_SIDE: Record<TransactionType, 'debited' \| 'credited' \| 'initiator' \| null>` plus one `soleParticipant(transaction, side)` helper in a new `shared/domain/participants.ts`. `reversal.ts:45-51` and `round-tracking.ts:24-30` are a jscpd clone of each other.                                                |
| `src/utils/transaction-history.ts:19` (20 cases, complexity 67)                                                                 | One description string per transaction type                                                                                                        | `const DESCRIBERS: Record<TransactionType, (context: DescribeContext) => string>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.ts:194` (18 cases) and `:310` (16 cases)                                                      | `buildRequest` and `previewBalances`                                                                                                               | `Record<ActionType, (input) => PlayerTransactionRequest \| null>` and `Record<PlayerTransactionRequest['type'], (request, context) => Map<string, number>>`. See 3.2 for moving the preview onto the shared domain operations.                                                                                                   |
| `worker/validation/api-validation.ts:355` (18 cases)                                                                            | Per-type request parser                                                                                                                            | `const transactionParsers: Record<TransactionRequestType, (body) => PlayerTransactionRequest>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `worker/services/banking-service.ts:319` (12 cases), `:1333` (7 cases)                                                          | Command dispatch to domain operations                                                                                                              | `const operations: Record<…, (context) => BankingOperationResult>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `src/utils/calculator.ts:85` (10 cases, cognitive 57)                                                                           | Reducer                                                                                                                                            | One function per action in `const reducers: { [K in CalculatorAction['type']]: (state, action) => CalculatorState }`.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `shared/domain/game-summary.ts:69-82`                                                                                           | `transaction.type === 'PLAYER_TO_BANK' \|\| …` membership chains (5 and 4 disjuncts)                                                               | `const PAID_TO_BANK = new Set<TransactionType>([…])` and `RECEIVED_FROM_BANK`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `src/utils/money.ts:18`, `src/utils/calculator.ts:59` (4 cases each)                                                            | Small, exhaustive                                                                                                                                  | Leave as is.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### 2.3 Conditionals nested deeper than 3 levels

| Location                              | Measured depth | Proposed replacement                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | -------------: | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `shared/domain/game-summary.ts:64-65` |              4 | Guard clause: `if (metric === undefined) continue;` before `metric.transactionCount += 1`, which removes one level. |
| `src/utils/local-qr.ts:121`           |              4 | Extract `applyMask(matrix, mask)` for the inner two loops.                                                          |
| `src/utils/local-qr.ts:235-237`       |            4-5 | Extract `runPenalty(line: boolean[])` and call it per row and per column (`score` has cognitive complexity 39).     |
| `worker/routes/api-router.ts:585`     |              4 | Disappears with the route table (`transactionMatch` → method → `REVERSAL` → else).                                  |

Ternaries nested 3 or more deep (AST scan, 15 sites): `src/pages/game/BankingDialog.tsx:277, 320, 354, 690, 828, 952`; `src/pages/GamePage.tsx:417, 806`; `src/App.tsx:512`; `src/components/DiceRoller.tsx:204`; `src/pages/JoinGamePage.tsx:159`; `src/pages/game/AuctionDialog.tsx:133`; `src/pages/game/GameHeader.tsx:117`; `shared/contracts/live.ts:120`; `worker/routes/api-router.ts:841`. Concrete replacements:

- `BankingDialog.tsx:272-279` (`activeBuildGroup`): a named function `pickBuildGroup(buildGroup, quickGroup, groupIds)` with three early returns.
- `BankingDialog.tsx:316-324` (amount by action): `const TILE_AMOUNT: Partial<Record<ActionType, (tile) => number>>`.
- `BankingDialog.tsx:350-356` (`effectiveTargetId`): `targetFor[action]?.(state) ?? targetId ?? soleRecipient?.id`.
- `GamePage.tsx:413-422` (`activeNav`): an ordered array `[[toolsOpen, 'tools'], [activityOpen, 'activity'], …].find(([open]) => open)?.[1] ?? null`.
- `api-router.ts:838-846` (rate-limit category): `const RATE_LIMITS: Array<{ method, pattern, limit }>` and `.find()`.

### 2.4 Nested loops

73 sites iterate at depth 2 or more (statement loops plus iteration callbacks such as `.map`, `.find`, `.some`). All are listed in [Appendix A5](#a5-nested-iteration-73-sites-at-depth-2-or-more). Grouped by what to do:

| Pattern                                                                      | Sites                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Proposed replacement                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Player or tile lookup by id inside a loop (`.map › .find`, `for-of › .find`) | `shared/domain/game-awards.ts:67-68`, `shared/domain/game-summary.ts:92-93`, `shared/domain/property.ts:488`, `src/components/game/BalanceChart.tsx:25`, `src/pages/game/AuctionDialog.tsx:70`, `src/pages/game/LoanFields.tsx:30`, `src/pages/game/PaymentInbox.tsx:75, 115`, `src/pages/game/WalletsSection.tsx:127`, `src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.ts:363`, `src/pages/game/BankruptcyDialog.tsx:93`, `src/utils/property-view.ts:374`, `worker/routes/api-router.ts:429`, `worker/services/banking-service.ts:752, 1199, 1277` | Build `new Map(players.map((p) => [p.id, p]))` once outside the loop (one shared `indexById` helper in `shared/domain`). |
| Set membership inside a loop (`.filter › .some`, `.some › .some`)            | `shared/domain/reversal.ts:151-154`, `src/pages/GamePage.tsx:344`, `src/pages/game/BankingDialog.tsx:626`, `src/pages/CreateGamePage.tsx:174, 706`, `src/utils/property-view.ts:218`, `worker/game-session.ts:744`, `worker/routes/api-router.ts:464, 949`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Precompute a `Set` and use `.has()`.                                                                                     |
| Three-level `.map` in JSX                                                    | `src/components/game/PropertyPicker.tsx:69, 86`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Extract a `GroupTiles` child component.                                                                                  |
| QR matrix loops                                                              | `src/utils/local-qr.ts` (15 sites, two at depth 3: `:119`, `:233`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Extract per-row and per-column helpers (see 2.3); the matrix nesting itself is inherent to QR encoding.                  |
| Nested `for-of` aggregation                                                  | `shared/domain/game-awards.ts:120-122`, `shared/domain/game-summary.ts:60`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Extract `accumulateParticipant(metrics, participant, isLoan)`.                                                           |

The 110 clones that involve tests are concentrated in `worker/game-session.test.ts` (19 internal clones, 253 lines), `worker/routes/api-router.test.ts` (8 clones, 173 lines), `bank-loan.sqlite.test.ts` ↔ `loan.sqlite.test.ts` (5 clones, 110 lines) and the SQLite fixtures shared by `auction`, `board`, `jackpot`, `mortgage`, `game-statistics` and `transaction-reversal` `*.sqlite.test.ts` (one 43-56 line seeding block each). A shared `worker/repositories/test-support/seed-game.ts` builder would remove most of them.

#### What the surviving mutants mean

String-literal mutants make up 565 of the 2,932 undetected mutants (survived plus no coverage). They are mostly error and log message text and are low value, so Appendix B counts them per line instead of listing them row by row. The survivors that matter, grouped by the behaviour that is not checked:

| Behaviour not checked                                                    | Surviving mutation (path:line)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Test that should catch it                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Statistics skip a reversed transaction and its reversal (a product rule) | `transaction.type === 'REVERSAL' \|\| …` → `false` and `reversesTransactionId == null` → `true` (`shared/domain/game-summary.ts:37, 48, 49`); `amount > 0` → `>= 0` (`shared/domain/game-awards.ts:47`)                                                                 | `shared/domain/game-summary.test.ts` and `shared/domain/game-awards.test.ts`: a payment plus its `REVERSAL` must leave every total unchanged |
| Bank-loan interest rounds half up                                        | `product + 50` → `product - 50` (`shared/domain/bank-loan.ts:14`)                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `shared/domain/banking.bank-loan.test.ts`: principal 5 at 10% gives 1, principal 4 at 10% gives 0                                            |
| A bid equal to the available funds is allowed                            | `available < bid` → `<=` (`shared/domain/auction.ts:131`); `reserve > 0` → `>= 0` (`:147`)                                                                                                                                                                              | `shared/domain/auction.test.ts`                                                                                                              |
| Bankruptcy to an inactive creditor or to oneself is refused              | the whole `if (creditor !== null) { ensureActive(creditor); ensureDifferentPlayers(…) }` block emptied (`shared/domain/banking.ts:1365`)                                                                                                                                | `shared/domain/banking.property.test.ts` or a new `banking.bankruptcy.test.ts`                                                               |
| Selling buildings is refused when not allowed                            | `sellBlocker` body emptied (`shared/domain/property.ts:265`), seen from the Node tests                                                                                                                                                                                  | `shared/domain/banking.building.test.ts`                                                                                                     |
| Error `details` the client reads                                         | 79 undetected `ObjectLiteral` mutants in run 1 replace `details: { … }` with `{}`, e.g. `shared/domain/banking.ts:154, 365-466`, `shared/domain/property.ts:13-108`, `shared/domain/auction.ts:35-90`                                                                   | The existing refusal tests, with `toMatchObject({ code, details })` instead of `toThrow(ErrorClass)`                                         |
| Request validation bounds                                                | `players.length < 1 \|\| players.length > 6` → `false` (`worker/validation/api-validation.ts:63`); 15 `Regex` survivors in the same file                                                                                                                                | `worker/validation/api-validation.test.ts`: 0 and 7 players, and one invalid input per regex                                                 |
| Money unit rounding at the boundary                                      | `power <= 12` → `< 12`, `candidate >= MINIMUM_UNIT` → `>` (`shared/domain/game-rules.ts:26, 29`)                                                                                                                                                                        | `shared/domain/game-rules.test.ts`                                                                                                           |
| Zero-delta participants (trades) are neither payer nor recipient         | `balanceDelta < 0` → `<= 0` and `> 0` → `>= 0` (`shared/domain/reversal.ts:45-46`, `shared/domain/round-tracking.ts:24-25`, `src/utils/transaction-history.ts:13-14`)                                                                                                   | `reversal.test.ts`, `round-tracking.test.ts`, `transaction-history.test.ts` with a `PROPERTY_TRANSFER` where one side has delta 0            |
| The review screen names source and destination                           | the bodies of `actionDescription` (`src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.ts:397`), `sourceFor` (`:423`) and `transactionLabel` (`:466`) emptied                                                                                                                             | `src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.test.ts`                                                                                                   |
| Live events merge optional state                                         | `event.loans === undefined` → `false`, `event.bankLoanCooldowns === undefined` → `false`, `event.properties === undefined` → `true` (`src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.ts:122-127`)                                                                                     | `game-page-helpers.test.ts › applyLiveEvent` with events that carry, and that omit, each optional field                                      |
| The local QR is decodable                                                | 423 of 515 mutants survive in `src/utils/local-qr.ts`, including deleting `push(bits, byte, 8)` (`:47`) and every masking and placement loop. `local-qr.test.ts` only checks the size (49×49) and that the same input gives the same output.                            | `src/utils/local-qr.test.ts`: decode the matrix (for example with a QR decoder as a dev dependency) or compare it to a golden matrix         |
| A draft from another user, or a malformed one, is ignored                | `typeof value !== 'object' \|\| value === null` → `false`, `draft.userId === userId && …` → `true` (`src/pages/create-game-draft.ts:53, 56`)                                                                                                                            | a new `src/pages/create-game-draft.test.ts`                                                                                                  |
| A tile operation needs its registry row                                  | `if (tileId !== undefined) { … }` emptied, the "missing row" guard → `false` (`worker/services/banking-service.ts:353-356`)                                                                                                                                             | `worker/services/banking-service.*.test.ts` with a board game whose registry lacks the tile                                                  |
| Routes                                                                   | 399 `api-router.ts` mutants have no coverage, and 8 whole route bodies can be emptied without a failing test: summary `:416`, start `:449`, activity `:487`, auction GET `:526`, player transactions `:710`, `readCommandId` `:745`, the live-route origin `try` `:359` | `worker/routes/api-router.test.ts`                                                                                                           |

**Code that can be deleted without a test failing** (`BlockStatement` mutants, whose block is replaced by `{}`). These counts include only blocks that tests do execute: 4 in run 1 (`shared/domain/banking.ts:1365, 1506, 1556`, `shared/domain/property.ts:265`), 30 in run 2 (23 in `src/utils/local-qr.ts`, 3 in `src/pages/game/game-page-helpers.ts:397, 428, 466`, 2 in `src/utils/property-name.ts:10, 18`, and one each at `src/utils/property-view.ts:295` and `src/utils/money-input.ts:50`), and 9 in run 3 (8 in `worker/routes/api-router.ts`, 1 at `worker/services/banking-service.ts:353`). Counting blocks no test reaches as well, the totals are 22, 38 and 51.

The full list of surviving and uncovered mutants per file is in [Appendix B](#appendix-b-surviving-mutants).

### 4.3 Tests that cannot fail

AST scan of all 157 test files (1,431 `it`/`test` call sites; `it.each` expands to the 1,596 run-time tests):

| Check                                                                                         | Result                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Tests with no `expect` and no `expect*`/`assert*` helper or `.rejects`/`.resolves`            | **0**                                                                                                                           |
| Snapshot-only tests (`toMatchSnapshot`/`toMatchInlineSnapshot`)                               | **0** (no snapshots in the repo)                                                                                                |
| `it.skip`, `it.only`, `describe.skip`, `xit`, `it.todo`                                       | **0**                                                                                                                           |
| Tests whose every assertion is on a mock (`toHaveBeenCalled*`)                                | **28**                                                                                                                          |
| Tests whose every assertion is on a class name, inline style, `querySelector` or a call count | **10**                                                                                                                          |
| Tests that pass when the code under test is removed                                           | Measured with Stryker `BlockStatement` mutants (function body replaced by `{}`): see the counts per file in 4.2 and Appendix B. |

The 28 mock-only tests fall into two groups:

- **Callback-contract tests in UI components (20).** They assert that the right prop callback receives the right payload, for example `src/pages/game/BankingDialog.test.tsx:937` (mortgage at the card value), `:954`, `:1077`, `:1132`, `src/pages/CreateGamePage.test.tsx:170, 295`, `src/pages/JoinGamePage.test.tsx:26, 35, 99`, `src/pages/game/ReverseTransactionDialog.test.tsx:66, 133`, `src/components/game/GameBottomNav.test.tsx:53, 59`, `src/components/ui/Button.test.tsx:80`, `src/components/ui/Toggle.test.tsx:56`, `src/components/ui/selection-kit.test.tsx:37`, `src/pages/ProfilePage.test.tsx:54`, `src/pages/game/AuctionDialog.test.tsx:106`, `src/pages/game/BankingDialog.test.tsx:818, 845`. They can fail, but they check the component boundary only; none asserts what the user sees afterwards.
- **Service tests that assert the repository call instead of the outcome (8).** `worker/services/banking-service.rules.test.ts:64` and `:77` check `persist` was called with `amount: 400`/`200`, not the returned balances; `worker/services/banking-service.mortgage.test.ts:121`, `worker/services/banking-service.reversal.test.ts:191`, `worker/services/game-service.lifecycle.test.ts:101`, `worker/services/banking-service.loans.test.ts:337`. Two only assert that a query was **not** made, which is an implementation detail and breaks on a harmless refactor: `worker/services/banking-service.loans.test.ts:313` (`listActive` not called) and `worker/services/banking-service.mortgage.test.ts:181` ("do not load the mortgage list").

The 10 implementation-detail tests: `src/components/game/GameBottomNav.test.tsx:53, 59`, `src/components/game/TransactionRow.test.tsx:64`, `src/components/game/WalletMenu.test.tsx:8`, `src/components/ui/Button.test.tsx:55`, `src/components/ui/Notice.test.tsx:22`, `src/components/ui/SegmentedControl.test.tsx:41`, `src/components/ui/SheetActions.test.tsx:34`, `src/components/ui/selection-kit.test.tsx:15`, `src/pages/game/InviteDialog.test.tsx:55`. For the UI kit, class assertions are the contract of the design system (`docs/ui-design-system.md`), so they are acceptable there; `TransactionRow.test.tsx:64` and `InviteDialog.test.tsx:55` would be stronger as role/text assertions.

### 4.4 Untested behaviours

Each line cites the coverage report (uncovered lines or never-called functions), a surviving mutant, or a grep that found no test.

**Error paths**

- `worker/routes/api-router.ts`: no test reaches register (`:156-157`), verify-email (`:164-166`), password-reset confirm (`:172-174`), logout (`:177`), upgrade/email/verification/telemetry (`:237-261`), live join (`:292-299`), lobby player removal (`:384-388`), invitation revoke (`:402-405`), summary (`:419-430`), duplicate (`:438-447`), start (`:452-457`), finish (`:460-485`), activity paging (`:490-514`, `readActivityCursor` `:774`), favourite amounts (`:517-522`), auctions GET/POST/bid (`:529-557`), bankruptcy (`:561-575`), payment-request list and actions (`:611-649`), and `failure()` (`:933-936`).
- `worker/game-session.ts`: `acceptConnection` (`:92`), `webSocketMessage` (`:121`), `webSocketClose` (`:134`), `broadcastPresence` (`:644`), `finishGame` (`:742`), `tryJson` (`:789`), `isHeartbeat` (`:796`) are never called; authorization branches `:459-480` are not executed.
- `worker/services/banking-service.ts:207-218`: replaying a command whose transaction already exists (the idempotency path) is never executed. `listPaymentRequests`, `declinePaymentRequest`, `cancelPaymentRequest`, `getTradeRequest`, `listPlayerTransactions` and `resolvePaymentRequest` are never called by unit tests.
- `worker/services/auth-service.ts`: `update`, `verifyEmail`, `addEmailToLegacyAccount`, `cleanupExpired`, `exportAccount` and `deleteAccount` are never called.
- `worker/index.ts` (`fetch`, `scheduled` cleanup, `apiOperation`), `worker/services/transactional-email.ts`, `worker/repositories/{auth-token,privacy,game-completion}-repository.ts`: 0% lines.
- Client error mapping: no test covers the 27 unmapped codes in 3.2; `src/i18n/api-errors.ts` falls back silently.

**Empty states.** No test asserts `noPendingActivity` (`src/pages/ActivityScreen.tsx:259`), `noPaymentRequests` (`src/pages/game/PaymentInbox.tsx:71`), `noWinner` (`src/pages/StatisticsScreen.tsx:83`), `propertyNoMatches` (`src/pages/PropertyScreen.tsx:193`) or `noSavedGames` (`src/pages/SavedGamesPage.tsx:254`), by key or by English text. `noActivity` and `propertyEmptyMine` are tested.

**Boundary amounts**

- Zero: `formatMoney(0, 'K')` is the only zero case for formatting (`src/utils/money.test.ts:17`).
- Negative: `formatMoney` with a negative value (`src/utils/money.ts:16-17`, renders `$-1 000`) has no test; only `formatMoneyDelta(-1000)` does (`money.test.ts:20`).
- Very large values and formatting overflow: `formatThousands` uses `Math.abs(value).toString()` (`src/utils/money.ts:5-7`), which switches to exponent notation from 1e21 and keeps decimals for non-integers. No test passes a non-integer, `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` or 1e21. Validation caps amounts at safe integers (`worker/validation/api-validation.ts:319, 603`), so the risk is display-only.
- The new 4,000,000 bank-to-player cap is tested only in the domain (`shared/domain/banking.test.ts:128-129`). The UI check (`src/pages/game/BankingDialog.tsx:399`, message at `:1251`), the HTTP 400 path and the client message `errorBankToPlayerLimitExceeded` have no test (grep for `BANK_TO_PLAYER_MAX`, `4_000_00`, `4000001` in test files finds only `banking.test.ts`).
- Surviving boundary mutants: interest rounding `product + 50` → `product - 50` (`shared/domain/bank-loan.ts:14`); bid exactly equal to available funds, `available < bid` → `<=` (`shared/domain/auction.ts:131`); auction reserve `reserve > 0` → `>= 0` (`shared/domain/auction.ts:147`); money-unit rounding `power <= 12` → `< 12` and `candidate >= MINIMUM_UNIT` → `>` (`shared/domain/game-rules.ts:26, 29`); player count `players.length < 1 || players.length > 6` → `false` (`worker/validation/api-validation.ts:63`).

**Concurrent updates.** Repository-level races are covered against real SQLite (for example `worker/repositories/bank-loan.sqlite.test.ts › allows one active bank loan per player and rolls a racing second one back`, and the compare-and-set tests in the `board`, `jackpot`, `auction`, `loan` and `mortgage` `*.sqlite.test.ts` files). Not covered: the client's live-version gap resync (`src/pages/GamePage.tsx:230-237, 263` uncovered), command replay in the service (`banking-service.ts:207-218`), and the Durable Object's message path (`webSocketMessage` never called).

**Offline and reconnect.** `src/App.tsx` (online/offline listeners `:143-247`) is at 0%. The reconnect backoff in `src/pages/GamePage.tsx:268-282` is not executed (`:270-271`, `:280` uncovered). No test file in `src/` mentions `online`, `offline`, `onclose` or `reconnect` (grep).

**Worker and D1 failure paths.** Simulated D1 failures appear in 10 test files (`mockRejected*`, `rejects.toThrow`, `DATABASE_ERROR`), mostly repository CHECK rollbacks. Not covered: `logError` (`worker/services/error-handler.ts:43`, never called); the `PersistenceConsistencyError` path (`banking-service.ts:295`); the client message for `DATABASE_ERROR` (unmapped, see 3.2).

The order is deliberate: each refactor in (B) is preceded by the tests in (A) that protect it, listed in its "Protected by" column. Sizes: S ≤ half a day, M ≤ 2 days, L > 2 days.

Suggested sequence: A1–A8 (domain and helper tests, all S/M), then B1–B5 and B8–B9, then A9–A16, then B6, B7, B10, B11, and last the L items B12–B15, each after its protecting tests are green and, where listed, after a fresh Stryker run on the touched file.

## Could not verify

- **Stryker against the repo's own Vitest 5.0.0.** `@stryker-mutator/vitest-runner` 10.0.0 (the latest release) ran **0 tests per mutant** under Vitest 5.0.0, so every mutant was reported as surviving. A probe on `src/utils/money.ts` reported 50 of 50 mutants surviving with `perTest` coverage, and again with coverage analysis off. A first full run with that setup was stopped and its numbers discarded. All mutation results in this report come from the scratch clone with **Vitest 4.1.12** swapped in. The full suite passes there too: 158 files and 1,596 tests, the same as Vitest 5.0.0. The runner's incompatibility with Vitest 5 is the reason; until the runner supports Vitest 5, mutation testing cannot run against the repo's pinned toolchain.
- **Stryker with the repo's `test.projects` setup.** The runner was given two flat configs instead, one with Node for `shared/**` and `worker/**` tests and one with jsdom plus `src/test/setup.ts` for `src/**` tests. As a result, a mutant in `shared/domain` that only a `src/` test would kill counts as survived in the Node run, and the other way round. Scores are therefore a lower bound for files that both environments exercise.
- **Mutation scope not covered.** These files were not mutated, so no score exists for them: `worker/game-session.ts`, every `worker/repositories/*`, `worker/services/*` other than `banking-service.ts` and `auction-service.ts`, `shared/contracts/live.ts`, and every `.tsx` component and page.
- **Equivalent mutants were not triaged by hand.** For example, `shared/domain/mortgage.ts:26` (`toLowerCase` → `toUpperCase`) is almost certainly equivalent for the case-insensitive name check. The scores are the raw Stryker numbers.
- **"Tests that pass when the code under test is deleted"** was measured only as surviving `BlockStatement` mutants inside the mutated scope above, not for the rest of the code base.
- **Runtime.** The repo requires Node 22 (`.nvmrc`, `engines`); only Node 24.19.0 was installed. Every number here was produced on Node 24.
- **Not run:** `npm run test:d1`, `npm run test:visual` and `npm run test:e2e` (the e2e suite needs `E2E_BASE_URL` against staging). Coverage therefore excludes Playwright and real Durable Object / workerd behaviour. Offline, reconnect and multi-tab concurrency in a real browser were not exercised.
- **jscpd scope.** Only `src`, `worker` and `shared` were scanned; `e2e/`, `scripts/` and `migrations/` were not.
- **Not measured:** bundle size, render performance and D1 query plans.

Files with more than 120 survived mutants are condensed to one row per source line.
